    PROPOSED FINAL DIVIDEND                                                                建議末期股息

REMUNERATION SYSTEM                                           薪酬訂立制度

The Group ’ s remuneration policy has been determined         本集團的薪酬政策是根據其表現，本地的
based on its performance, changes in the local consumption    消費水平變化和人力資源市場競爭狀況
power and competition in human resources market. The          釐定。該釐定的薪酬政策作為聘任不同崗
remuneration policy so determined has become the basis        位僱員薪資水平的基準。每位僱員的薪資
of determining the salary level of employees recruited        視乎僱員的表現、能力、任職條件及本公
for different positions. The salary of each employee is       司的預定薪資標準而定。董事的酬金是由
determined according to the employee ’ s performance,         薪 酬 委 員 會 經 參 考 本 公 司 的 經 營 業 績、
ability, employment conditions and the salary standards set   董事個人表現及市場競爭情況的基礎上
by the Company. Remuneration of directors is proposed         制定薪酬方案，並經股東於應屆股東週年
by the Remuneration Committee with reference to the           大會上授權董事會釐定。
operating results of the Company, personal performance
of the directors and market competition. The proposed
remuneration of directors is proposed by the Board subject
to approval by shareholders at forthcoming annual general
meeting.

     in the PRC and the United States. Assets, liabilities and            為主。中國境內資產、負債及交易以人民

     overseas assets and transactions are mainly denominated              值，外匯風險主要來源於未償還的外幣借
     in US dollars. Foreign exchange risk mainly arises from              貸。今年以來，本集團已採用了外幣對沖

     Since this year, the Group has adopted foreign currency              值的目標在於使預計未來可能發生的償
     hedging instruments to achieve better foreign exchange               還外幣負債需要的人民幣支出的波動性
     risk management. The objective of the hedge is to minimise           最小化。本集團的風險管理政策是在考慮
     the volatility of the RMB expenditures expected to be                合適的對沖工具以及對沖的成本後，部分
     incurred in the future to meet foreign currency liabilities.         對沖外幣預測現金流量。本集團通過外匯
     The Group’s risk management policy is to partially hedge the         結 構 性 衍 生 金 融 合 同 來 對 沖 外 匯 風 險。
     forecasted cash flows in foreign currencies by considering           截至二零一九年十二月三十一日止十二
     the appropriate hedging instruments and costs of hedging.            個月，本集團並無因匯率波動而遇到重大
     The Group uses foreign exchange structured derivative                困難或自身的營運資金受到影響。
     financial contracts to hedge its foreign currency risk. For

AGREEMENT WITH LANHAI BANK

On 20 December 2017, the Company and 威海藍海銀行股                        二 零 一 九 年 十 二 月 二 十 日，本 公 司 與 威
份有限公司 (Weihai Lanhai Banking Corporation*) (“Lanhai                 海藍海銀行股份有限公司（「藍海銀行」）
Bank ” ) renewed and entered into the deposit services              重續及訂立存款服務協議，據此，本集團
agreement, pursuant to which the Group utilizes the deposit         使用藍海銀行提供之存款服務。根據存款
services offered by Lanhai Bank. Pursuant to the deposit            服務協議，藍海銀行向本集團提供存款服

RISK MANAGEMENT AND INTERNAL                                     風險管理及內部監控
CONTROL

The Board acknowledges that it is their responsibility for       董事會承認其須對風險管理及內部監控
the risk management and internal control systems and             系統負責，並有責任檢討該等系統的有效
reviewing their effectiveness, and such systems are designed     性。該等系統旨在管理而非消除未能達成
to manage rather than eliminate the risk of failure to achieve   業務目標的風險。審核委員會協助董事會
business objectives. The Audit Committee assists the Board       履行其於本集團財務、營運、合規、風險
in fulfilling its oversight and corporate governance roles in    管理及內部監控以及內部審核功能方面
relation to the Group’s financial, operational, compliance,      的監管及企業管治角色。董事會有責任確
risk management and internal controls, and internal audit        保本集團維持健全有效的監控以保障其
functions. The Board has responsibility to ensure the Group      資產及股東投資。
maintains sound and effective controls to safeguard its assets
and shareholders’ investment.

The Group has internal control systems to formalize the          本集團擁有內部監控系統以規範本集團
risk management of the Group, to build up a standard and         的風險管理，建立標準及有效的風險管理
effective risk management system to manage risk and to           系統以管理風險及合理保證並無重大失
provide reasonable assurance against material misstatement       實陳述或損失。適當的政策及監控經已訂
or loss. Appropriate policies and controls have been designed    立及制定，以確保保障資產不會在未經許
and established to ensure the assets are safeguarded against     可下使用或處置，依從及遵守相關規則及
improper use or disposal, relevant rules and regulations         規例，根據相關會計標準及監管申報規定
are adhered to and complied with, reliable financial and         保存可靠的財務及會計記錄，以及適當地
accounting records are maintained in accordance with             管理可能影響本集團表現的主要風險。
relevant accounting standards and regulatory reporting
requirement and key risks that may impact on the Group’s
performance are appropriately managed.

     The Group has established an organizational structure with           本集團已建立一個具備明確職責等級及
     defined levels of responsibility and reporting procedures.           報告程序的組織架構。本公司已委聘獨立
     The Company has retained an independent professional                 專 業 公 司 擔 任 外 部 顧 問，協 助 本 公 司 對
     firm as external advisor to assist the Company to perform            本集團風險管理及內部監控系統進行評
     an assessment on risk management and internal control                估。我們擁有自己的內部審核部門履行內
     mechanism of the Group. We have our own Internal Audit               部審核功能規定。連同外部顧問的支持，
     Department to fulfil the requirement of internal audit               內部審核部門可幫助審核委員會及董事
     function. Together with the supports from the external               會，每年及持續地確定風險管理及內部監
     advisor, the internal audit function can facilitate the Audit        控系統的有效性。截至二零一九年十二月
     Committee and the Board of Directors in ascertaining the             三十一日止年度，本集團已進行年度風險
     effectiveness of the Group’s risk management and internal            評估，可識別本集團各主要業務分部的相
     control systems on an annual and ongoing basis. During the           關策略風險、經營風險、財務風險及合規
     year ended 31 December 2019, the Group has conducted an              風險。風險因素乃於本集團層面進行分析
     annual risk assessment which identified respective strategic         及綜合。根據風險評估結果及風險導向的
     risks, operational risks, financial risks and compliance risks       審核方法，本集團已制定一個三年審核計
     for each of the major business segment of the Group. Risk            劃，可據此將所識別之風險優先列入年度
     factors were analysed and consolidated at the Group level.           審核項目。本集團內部審核部門根據審核
     Based on the risk assessment results following a risk based          計劃進行年度檢討，旨在協助董事會及審
     methodology audit approach, a three-years’ audit plan was            核委員會評估本集團風險管理及內部監
     devised which prioritized the risks identified into annual           控系統的有效性。
     audit projects. The Group ’ s internal audit department
     performed the annual reviews in accordance with the
     audit plan with a view to assisting the Board and the Audit
     Committee to evaluate the effectiveness of the Group’s risk

to continue as a going concern, disclosing, as applicable,                              露與持續經營有關的事項，以及使用持續

concern basis of accounting unless the directors either intend                          清盤或停止經營，或別無其他實際的替代

     3. SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                   3. 主要會計政策

          The consolidated financial statements have been                       綜合財務報表乃按照香港會計師公
          prepared in accordance with HKFRSs issued by the                      會頒佈之香港財務報告準則編製。
          HKICPA. In addition, the consolidated financial                       此 外，綜 合 財 務 報 表 載 有 香 港 聯 合
          statements include applicable disclosures required by                 交易所有限公司證券上市規則（「上
          the Rules Governing the Listing of Securities on the                  市規則」）及香港公司條例規定的適
          Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited (“Listing Rules”)                 用披露。
          and by the Hong Kong Companies Ordinance.

          The consolidated financial statements have been                       除於各報告期末若干金融工具按公
          prepared on the historical cost basis except for certain              平 值 計 量 外，綜 合 財 務 報 表 乃 以 歷
          financial instruments that are measured at fair values                史成本為基礎編製。
          at the end of each reporting period, as explained in the
          accounting policies set out below.

          Historical cost is generally based on the fair value of the           歷史成本一般以換取貨品及服務所
          consideration given in exchange for goods and services.               作出代價之公平值為基準。

          Fair value is the price that would be received to sell                 公平值指市場參與者之間於計量日
          an asset or paid to transfer a liability in an orderly                 期進行的有序交易中出售一項資產
          transaction between market participants at the                         所收取的價格或轉移一項負債所支
          measurement date, regardless of whether that price is                  付 的 價 格，無 論 該 價 格 為 直 接 觀 察
          directly observable or estimated using another valuation               得出或採用其他估值技術作出的估
          technique. In estimating the fair value of an asset or a               計。在 對 資 產 或 負 債 的 公 平 值 作 出
          liability, the Group takes into account the characteristics            估 計 時，本 集 團 考 慮 了 市 場 參 與 者
          of the asset or liability if market participants would                 於計量日期為該資產或負債進行定
          take those characteristics into account when pricing                   價 時 將 會 考 慮 的 該 等 特 徵。在 該 等
          the asset or liability at the measurement date. Fair                   綜合財務報表中計量及╱或披露的
          value for measurement and/or disclosure purposes in                    公 平 值 均 按 此 基 礎 予 以 確 定，惟 香
          these consolidated financial statements is determined                  港 財 務 報 告 準 則 第2號以 股 份 為 基
          on such a basis, except for share-based payment                       準付款 範圍內的以股份為基礎付款
          transactions that are within the scope of HKFRS 2                      交 易、根 據 香 港 財 務 報 告 準 則 第16
          Share-based Payment , leasing transactions that are                    號（ 自二零一九年一月一日起）或香
          accounted for in accordance with HKFRS 16 (since 1                     港會計準則第17號入賬的租賃交易
          January 2019) or HKAS 17 (before application of HKFRS                 （ 於 應 用 香 港 財 務 報 告 準 則 第16號
          16), and measurements that have some similarities to                   前）、以及與公平值類似但並非公平
          fair value but are not fair value, such as net realisable              值的計量（ 例如，香港會計準則第2

          Basis of consolidation                                                綜合基準


          The consolidated financial statements incorporate                     綜合財務報表包含本公司以及本公
          the financial statements of the Company and entities                  司 及 其 附 屬 公 司 所 控 制 的 實 體（ 包
          (including structured entities) controlled by the                     括結構性實體）的財務報表。當本公
          Company and its subsidiaries. Control is achieved when                司符合以下情況，即取得控制權：
          the Company:

          •    has power over the investee;                                     •   有權控制被投資方；

          •    is exposed, or has rights, to variable returns from              •   因其參與被投資方業務而獲得
               its involvement with the investee; and                               或有權獲得可變回報；及

          •    has the ability to use its power to affect its returns.          •   有能力以其權力影響其回報。

          The Group reassesses whether or not it controls an                    倘有事實及情況顯示上述三項控制
          investee if facts and circumstances indicate that there               權 要 素 有 一 項 或 多 項 出 現 變 動，則
          are changes to one or more of the three elements of                   本集團會重新評估其是否控制被投
          control listed above.                                                 資方。

          Consolidation of a subsidiary begins when the Group                   附屬公司於本集團取得附屬公司的
          obtains control over the subsidiary and ceases when                   控 制 權 時 開 始 綜 合 入 賬，並 於 本 集
          the Group loses control of the subsidiary. Specifically,              團喪失對附屬公司的控制權時終
          income and expenses of a subsidiary acquired or                       止。特別是，年內收購或出售附屬公
          disposed of during the year are included in the                       司的收入及開支自本集團取得控制
          consolidated statement of profit or loss and other                    權當日起直至本集團不再控制附屬
          comprehensive income from the date the Group gains                    公司之日止於綜合損益及其他全面
          control until the date when the Group ceases to control               收益表列賬。
          the subsidiary.

          Profit or loss and each item of other comprehensive                   損益及各其他全面收入項目歸屬於
          income are attributed to the owners of the                            本 公 司 擁 有 人 及 非 控 股 權 益。附 屬
          Company and to the non-controlling interests. Total                   公司的全面收入總額會歸屬於本公
          comprehensive income of subsidiaries is attributed to                 司 擁 有 人 及 非 控 股 權 益，即 使 其 將
          the owners of the Company and to the non-controlling                  導致非控股權益為赤字結餘。

   A contract asset represents the Group ’ s right to                               合約資產指本集團就向客戶換取本
   consideration in exchange for goods or services that                             集團已轉讓的商品或服務收取代價
   the Group has transferred to a customer that is not                              的權利（ 尚未成為無條件）。其根據
   yet unconditional. It is assessed for impairment in                              香港財務報告準則第9號評估減值。
   accordance with HKFRS 9. In contrast, a receivable                               相 反，應 收 款 項 指 本 集 團 收 取 代 價
   re p re s e n t s t h e G ro u p ’ s u n c o n d i t i o n a l r i g h t t o     的 無 條 件 權 利，即 代 價 僅 隨 時 間 推
   consideration, i.e. only the passage of time is required                         移即成為到期支付。
   before payment of that consideration is due.

   A contract liability represents the Group’s obligation                           合約負債指本集團因已自客戶收取
   to transfer goods or services to a customer for which                            代價（ 或到期的代價金額），而須向
   the Group has received consideration (or an amount of                            客戶轉讓商品或服務之責任。
   consideration is due) from the customer.

   A contract asset and a contract liability relating to the                        與相同合約有關的合約資產及合約
   same contract are accounted for and presented on a net                           負債按淨額入賬及呈列。
   basis.

  Principal versus agent                                                            主事人與代理人

   When another party is involved in providing goods or                             倘向客戶提供商品或服務涉及另一
   services to a customer, the Group determines whether                             方，本 集 團 會 釐 定 其 承 諾 性 質 是 否
   the nature of its promise is a performance obligation                            為其本身提供指定商品或服務的履
   to provide the specified goods or services itself (i.e.                          約責任（ 即本集團為主事人），抑或
   the Group is a principal) or to arrange for those goods                          是安排另一方提供該等商品或服務
   or services to be provided by the other party (i.e. the                         （ 即本集團為代理人）。
   Group is an agent).

   The Group is a principal if it controls the specified good                       倘本集團於向客戶轉移指定商品或
   or service before that good or service is transferred to a                       服 務 前 控 制 該 商 品 或 服 務，則 為 主
   customer.                                                                        事人。

   the lease transfer substantially all the risks and rewards     資產擁有權附帶的絕大部分風險及

   lessee, the contract is classified as a finance lease. All     類 為 融 資 租 賃。所 有 其 他 租 賃 乃 分
   other leases are classified as operating leases.               類為經營租賃。

   at amounts equal to net investments in the leases,             等 於 租 賃 淨 投 資，並 使 用 各 個 租 賃
   measured using the interest rate implicit in the               中 隱 含 的 利 率 計 量。初 始 直 接 成 本
   respective leases. Initial direct costs (other than those     （ 製造商或交易出租人所致之成本除
   incurred by manufacturer or dealer lessors) are included       外）包 括 在 租 賃 淨 投 資 的 初 始 計 量
   in the initial measurement of the net investments in           中。利息收入被分配至會計期間，以
   the leases. Interest income is allocated to accounting         反映本集團有關租賃的未償還淨投
   periods so as to reflect a constant periodic rate of           資的固定定期收益率。
   return on the Group’s net investment outstanding in
   respect of the leases.

   Rental income from operating leases is recognised in           經營租約的租金收入以直線法按有
   profit or loss on a straight-line basis over the term          關 租 約 年 期 於 損 益 中 確 認。於 磋 商
   of the relevant lease. Initial direct costs incurred in        及安排經營租約時引致的初步直接

   Government grants                                              政府補助

   Government grants are not recognised until there is            在合理地保證本集團會遵守政府補

   a systematic basis over the periods in which the Group         由補助擬補償的相關成本確認為開

   the grants are intended to compensate. Specifically,           言，主要條件為本集團應購買、興建
   government grants whose primary condition is that the          或以其他方式收購非流動資產的政
   Group should purchase, construct or otherwise acquire          府補助乃於綜合財務狀況表確認為
   non-current assets are recognised as deferred income           遞延收入並且在有關資產的可使用
   in the consolidated statement of financial position and        年期內有系統及合理地轉移至損益
   transferred to profit or loss on a systematic and rational     內。
   basis over the useful lives of the related assets.

   Government grants that are receivable as compensation          用作補償本集團已產生支出或虧損
   for expenses or losses already incurred or for the             或旨在為本集團提供即時財務資助

   Group with no future related costs are recognised              助，乃於應收期間於損益內確認。

3. SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                3. 主要會計政策（ 續）
   (continued)


   Taxation                                                         稅項


   Income tax expense represents the sum of the tax                 所得稅開支指現時應付的稅項及遞
   currently payable and deferred tax.                              延稅項總和。

   The tax currently payable is based on taxable profit             現時應付的稅項以年內的應納稅溢
   for the year. Taxable profit differs from ‘profit before         利 為 基 礎。由 於 於 其 他 年 度 應 納 稅
   tax because of income or expense that are taxable                或可扣稅的收支及不應納稅或不可
   or deductible in other years and items that are never            扣稅的項目，應納稅溢利有別於「除
   taxable or deductible. The Group’s liability for current         稅前溢利」。本集團目前的稅務責任
   tax is calculated using tax rates that have been enacted         乃採用報告期末已製定或大致上已
   or substantively enacted by the end of the reporting             製定的稅率計算。
   period.

   Deferred tax is recognised on temporary differences              遞延稅項就綜合財務報表內資產及
   between the carrying amounts of assets and liabilities           負債的賬面值與用以計算應納稅溢
   in the consolidated financial statements and the                 利的相關稅基的暫時差額而確認。
   corresponding tax bases used in the computation of               遞延稅項負債一般就所有應納稅暫
   taxable profit. Deferred tax liabilities are generally           時 差 額 確 認。倘 有 應 納 稅 溢 利 將 可
   recognised for all taxable temporary differences.                用以抵銷可動用可扣稅暫時差額，
   Deferred tax assets are generally recognised for all             遞延稅項資產一般就所有可扣稅暫
   deductible temporary difference to the extent that it is         時 差 額 確 認。倘 暫 時 差 額 來 自 商 譽
   probable that taxable profits will be available against          或來自一項不會影響應納稅溢利或
   which those deductible temporary differences can be              會計溢利的交易的其他資產及負債
   utilised. Such assets and liabilities are not recognised         的初步確認（ 業務合併除外），則該
   if the temporary difference arises from goodwill or              等資產及負債不會予以確認。此外，
   from the initial recognition (other than in a business           倘 初 步 確 認 商 譽 產 生 暫 時 差 額，則
   combination) of other assets and liabilities in a                遞延稅項發債予以確認。
   transaction that affects neither the taxable profit nor
   the accounting profit. In addition, deferred tax liabilities
   are not recognised if the temporary difference arises
   from the initial recognition of goodwill.

   Deferred tax liabilities are recognised for taxable              遞延稅項負債乃按因於附屬公司及
   temporary differences associated with investments in             聯營公司之投資而產生的應課稅暫
   subsidiaries and an associate, except where the Group is         時 差 額 而 確 認，惟 若 本 集 團 可 令 暫
   able to control the reversal of the temporary difference         時差額撥回及暫時差額有可能未必
   and it is probable that the temporary difference will            於 可 見 將 來 撥 回 之 情 況 除 外。與 該
   not reverse in the foreseeable future. Deferred tax              等投資及權益相關的可扣稅暫時差
   assets arising from deductible temporary differences             額所產生的遞延稅項資產僅於可能
   associated with such investments and interests are only          有足夠應課稅溢利可以使用暫時差
   recognised to the extent that it is probable that there          額的益處且預計於可見將來可以撥
   will be sufficient taxable profits against which to utilise      回時確認。
   the benefits of the temporary differences and they are
   expected to reverse in the foreseeable future.

           (v)   Measurement and recognition of ECL                              (v)   預期信貸損失之計量及確認

                 magnitude of the loss if there is a default) and                      大 小）及 違 約 時 風 險 敞 口 之 函
                 the exposure at default. The assessment of the                        數。違 約 概 率 及 違 約 虧 損 之 評
                 probability of default and loss given default is                      估乃基於歷史數據按前瞻性資
                 based on historical data adjusted by forward-                         料 作 調 整。預 期 信 貸 損 失 的 預
                 looking information. Estimation of ECL reflects an                    估乃無偏概率加權平均金額，

                 is determined with the respective risks of default                    確定。
                 occurring as the weights.

                 contractual cash flows that are due to the Group                       據合約應付本集團的所有合約

                 that the Group expects to receive, discounted at                       現 金 流 之 間 的 差 額，並 按 初 始
                 the effective interest rate determined at initial                      確認時釐定的實際利率貼現。
                 recognition. For a lease receivable, the cash flows                    就 租 賃 應 收 款 項 而 言，釐 定 預
                 used for determining the ECL is consistent with the                    期信貸虧損所用的現金流與根

  Derecognition of financial assets                             終止確認金融資產


   The Group derecognises a financial asset only when           倘從資產收取現金流量之合約權利
   the contractual rights to the cash flows from the asset      已 到 期，或 金 融 資 產 已 轉 讓 且 本 集
   expire, or when it transfers the financial asset and         團已將其於金融資產擁有權之絕大
   substantially all the risks and rewards of ownership         部份風險及回報轉移至另一實體，
   of the asset to another entity. If the Group neither         則 金 融 資 產 將 被 取 消 確 認。倘 若 本
   transfers nor retains substantially all the risks and        集團並無轉讓或保留擁有權之絕大
   rewards of ownership and continues to control the            部 份 風 險 及 回 報，並 繼 續 控 制 已 轉
   transferred asset, the Group recognises its retained         讓 之 資 產，則 本 集 團 會 確 認 其 於 資
   interest in the asset and an associated liability for        產的保留權益及可能需要支付的相
   amounts it may have to pay. If the Group retains             關 負 債 款 項。倘 若 本 集 團 保 留 已 轉
   substantially all the risks and rewards of ownership         讓金融資產擁有權之絕大部份風險
   of a transferred financial asset, the Group continues        及 回 報，則 本 集 團 繼 續 確 認 金 融 資
   to recognise the financial asset and also recognises a       產並同時就所得收益確認抵押借
   collateralised borrowing for the proceeds received.          款。

   On derecognition of a financial asset measured at            當以攤銷成本計量的金融資產終止
   amortised cost, the difference between the asset’ s          確 認 時，資 產 的 賬 面 值 與 已 收 及 應
   carrying amount and the sum of the consideration             收代價款項總額之間的差額將於損
   received and receivable is recognised in profit or loss.     益中確認。

   On derecognition of an investment in a debt instrument       於終止確認分類為按公平值計入其
   classified as at FVTOCI, the cumulative gain or loss         他全面收益計量的債務工具投資
   previously accumulated in the FVTOCI reserve is              時，先 前 於 按 公 平 值 計 入 其 他 全 面
   reclassified to profit or loss.                              收益計量儲備中累計的累計收益或
                                                                虧損將重新分類至損益。

  Financial liabilities and equity                              金融負債及權益


  Classification as debt or equity                              分類為負債或權益


   Debt and equity instruments are classified as either         負債及股本工具乃根據合約安排的
   financial liabilities or as equity in accordance with        性質與金融負債及股本工具的定義
   the substance of the contractual arrangements and            分類為金融負債或權益。
   the definitions of a financial liability and an equity
   instrument.

           the issuer to make specified payments to reimburse the                項 債 務 工 具 之 條 款，因 特 定 債 務 人
           holder for a loss it incurs because a specified debtor                未能於到期日償還款項而需支付特
           fails to make payments when due in accordance with                    定款項以補償合約持有人所招致損
           the terms of a debt instrument. Financial guarantee                   失 之 一 項 合 約。財 務 擔 保 合 約 於 首
           contract liabilities are measured initially at their fair             次 確 認 時 以 其 公 平 值 計 量。於 首 次
           values. It is subsequently measured at the higher of:                 確認後則以下列較高者計量：

           •    the amount of the loss allowance determined in                   •   按 香 港 財 務 報 告 準 則 第9號 所
                accordance with HKFRS 9; and                                         確定虧損撥備金額；及

           •    the amount initially recognised less, where                      •   於首次確認之金額減於擔保期
                appropriate, cumulative amortisation recognised                      間之累計攤銷（ 如適用）。
                over the guarantee period.

           only when, the Group’ s obligations are discharged,                   消 或 屆 滿 時，本 集 團 便 會 終 止 確 認
           cancelled or have expired. The difference between the                 金 融 負 債。終 止 確 認 的 金 融 負 債 的
           carrying amount of the financial liability derecognised               賬面值與已付及應付代價之間的差

           year 2017 to 2019. In accordance with the “Notice                            稅務總局關於企業所得稅若干優惠

           on Enterprise Income Tax”, New and High Technical

      42. CAPITAL RISK MANAGEMENT                                          42. 資本風險管理

           The Group manages its capital to ensure that entities in              本集團其管理資本乃為確保本集團
           the Group will be able to continue as a going concern                 內 各 實 體 將 可 以 持 續 方 式 經 營，同
           while maximising the return to shareholders through                   時透過適當優化債務與權益結餘為
           the optimisation of the debt and equity balance. The                  股 東 帶 來 最 大 回 報。本 集 團 的 整 體
           Group’s overall strategy remains unchanged from prior                 策略與以往年度保持不變。
           year.

           The capital structure of the Group consists of net                     本集團的資本結構包括債務淨額
           debt (which includes borrowings net of cash and cash                  （ 包括扣除現金及現金等價物後之借
           equivalents) and equity attributable to owners of                      款）以及本公司擁有人應佔權益（ 包
           the Company (comprising issued share capital, share                    括已發行股本、股份溢價、儲備及保
           premium, reserves and retained profits).                               留溢利）。

           The directors of the Company review the capital                       本公司董事按半年基準對資本結構

           of fluctuations in cash flows. The management monitors                來 的 影 響。管 理 層 監 察 借 款 的 使 用
           the utilisation of borrowings and ensures compliance                  情況並確保符合貸款契約。
           with loan covenants.

           subject to change if changes in variable interest rates               金融負債之可變利率工具納入之數
           different to those estimates of interest rates determined             額會出現變動。
           at the end of the reporting period.

           date on which the Group can be required to pay. The                   利 息 及 本 金 現 金 流 量。因 利 息 流 為

49. EVENT AFTER THE REPORTING PERIOD                                    49. 報告期後事項

   Since the outbreak of the COVID-19 pandemic in China                   自COVID-19疫情於二零二零年一月
   and around the world from January 2020, governments                    在 中 國 爆 發 並 蔓 延 全 球，各 國 已 要
   in various countries have required enterprises to                      求 企 業 採 取 措 施 防 控 疫 情，包 括 旅
   implement measures to prevent and control the disease,                 遊限制、檢疫安排或暫停營運活動。
   including travel restrictions, quarantine arrangements                 其後的防控措施已對本集團的營運
   or suspension of operating activities. The subsequent                  造 成 一 定 影 響，因 為 本 集 團 的 業 務
   prevention and control measures have certain impact                    營運位於中國內地、美國、歐洲及其
   on the operation of the Group, as the Group’s business                 他多國。
   operations are located in Mainland China, United
   States, Europe and multiple other countries.

   Given the dynamic nature of these circumstances, the                   由 於 情 況 隨 時 變 動，本 公 司 董 事 認
   directors of the Company consider that the financial                   為，截 至 本 集 團 綜 合 財 務 報 表 獲 授
   effects on the Group’s consolidated financial statements               權 刊 發 之 日，未 能 合 理 評 估 對 有 關
   cannot be reasonably estimated as at the date of these                 財 務 報 表 的 財 務 影 響，但 預 期 將 影
   financial statements which are authorised for issue, but               響二零二零年上半年的綜合業績。
   are expected to affect the consolidated results for the
   first half year of 2020.
